<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将自动驾驶视为视觉问答任务，导致决策推理不可靠且可解释性差；同时缺乏异质传感器之间的有效多模态交互，限制了长尾驾驶场景下的鲁棒感知和可靠推理。

Method: 包含三个核心组件：(1) 亲和力引导的最优传输实现主-辅模态双向交互；(2) 分布一致模态迁移实现异质模态分布迁移与跨模态交互；(3) 多模态多轨迹规划配合面向感知的轨迹细化，以应对长尾驾驶场景。

Result: 在开环和闭环数据集上的实验结果表明，相比现有驾驶系统，该方法在安全性、长时域驾驶推理和道路场景感知方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 多模态交互与多轨迹规划优化的结合能够为可扩展的VLA自动驾驶系统提供更可靠、可解释且更安全的驾驶决策支持。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving by jointly integrating perception, reasoning, and decision making within a unified multimodal framework. However, most existing VLA models formulate end-to-end autonomous driving as a visual question answering task, leading to unreliable and less interpretable decision reasoning. In addition, they fail to establish effective multi-modal interaction across heterogeneous sensors, thereby limiting robust scene perception and reliable driving reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. To this end, we propose a robust VLA-based end-to-end autonomous driving system that combines multi-modality interaction with multi-trajectory planning and optimization, enabling more reliable, interpretable, and safer driving decisions. Our method comprises three core components: (1) Affinity-Guided Optimal Transport for main-auxiliary modality two-way interaction; (2) Distribution-Consistent Modality Transfer for heterogeneous modality distribution transfer and cross-modal interaction; (3) Multi-modal Multi-Trajectory Planning along with Perception-Oriented Trajectory Refinement for better driving decisions to long-tail driving scenarios. Experimental results in open-loop and closed-loop datasets demonstrate improvements in safety long-horizon driving reasoning and road scene perception over existing driving systems, highlighting the ability of our mutli-modality interaction and multi-trajectory planning and optimization for scalable VLA-based systems.

</details>


### [33] [CertVLA: Certified Defense against Physical Visual Attacks for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20791)
*Hui Lu,Zhijie Peng,Yuqi Lin,Zaijia Yang,Jiaming He,Shuhan Ye,Yi Yu,Hanwei Zhu,Bingquan Shen,Alex Kot,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CertVLA是一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的认证防御方法，能够在有界patch和纹理攻击下为闭环控制提供行为一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证patch防御方法仅针对离散标签，无法直接认证连续且时间相关的动作输出，而VLA策略又容易受到局部物理扰动的影响。

Method: 提出校准的行为一致动作区域和确定性覆盖掩码，确保至少一个被检查的预测无攻击；通过归一化动作分歧并校准max-min-max片段得分来提供有限样本覆盖保证；将查询级决策连接以扩展到完整闭环 rollout。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，CertVLA对patch攻击展示了经验和认证有效性，并在仿真中额外验证了纹理攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: CertVLA的认证不依赖于patch内容、生成方法和物理变换，对自适应攻击者具有理论保证，且能进一步保证任务成功。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are vulnerable to localized physical perturbations, yet existing certified patch defenses target discrete labels and cannot directly certify continuous, temporally correlated actions. We introduce CertVLA, a certified defense for closed-loop VLA control under bounded patch and texture attacks. CertVLA proposes a calibrated region of behaviorally consistent actions, while deterministic covering masks ensure that at least one checked prediction is attack-free. Specifically, CertVLA normalizes action disagreement by the benign variation of each mask pair and accepts a single-mask anchor only when it remains consistent under every second mask. It then calibrates the resulting max-min-max episode score to provide finite-sample clean coverage. Conjoining query-level decisions extends the action certificate to the complete closed-loop rollout. Furthermore, we prove that against any adaptive attacker satisfying the bounded-support threat model, every rollout certified by CertVLA executes only action chunks consistent with attack-erased clean predictions. Under dual-mask rollout correctness, this consistency certificate further guarantees task success. The certificate is independent of patch content, generation method, and physical transformation. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the empirical and certified effectiveness of CertVLA against patch attacks, with additional simulation validation on texture attacks.

</details>


### [34] [Just Noticeable Difference Modeling for Token Compression in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21247)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Rui Zhao,Jin Wang,Hanwei Zhu,Cong Zhang,Giuseppe Valenzise,Weisi Lin,Kin-Man Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Action-JND方法，将人类视觉系统中的"最小可觉差"（JND）概念扩展到具身智能体的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中，通过衡量token变化对下游动作偏差的影响来指导token压缩，确保压缩后不导致不可接受的行动偏差。在LIBERO基准上的实验表明，该方法在激进压缩比下显著提升了压缩可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token压缩方案（如token剪枝、KV缓存复用）主要依赖冗余度或重要性线索（视觉相似性、注意力分数、显著性）来指导压缩，但这些线索仅间接衡量了安全压缩的关键因素——一个token在被压缩后导致下游动作产生不可接受偏差的容忍度。具身智能体中，token直接影响延迟敏感的闭环机器人动作预测，因此需要一种直接以动作响应为导向的压缩准则。

Method: 提出Action-JND概念，将JND建模扩展到具身感知：通过VLA策略在闭环控制中的语言条件动作响应来定义可觉性。一个token的变更仅在诱发的动作偏差保持在容许范围内时被认为是可接受的。具体实现上，开发了一个轻量级token级JND估计器，在深度视觉特征空间中预测保持策略响应的最大可容忍扰动，生成行动容忍度分数，作为即插即用的压缩准则，用于旧KV复用和token剪枝，优先压缩行动容忍度高的token。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上使用OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT的实验表明，Action-JND一致性地提高了压缩可靠性，尤其是在激进压缩比下表现更为突出。

Conclusion: 通过将压缩准则从间接的视觉线索转向直接的动作响应容忍度度量，Action-JND为具身智能体的token压缩提供了一种更安全、更可靠的新范式，能够在保证闭环控制性能的同时实现高效压缩。

Abstract: Token compression has become a key technique for reducing the inference cost of large foundation models, with approaches such as token pruning and KV-cache reuse widely adopted in vision-language models and recently explored for embodied agents. In embodied agents, tokens not only support perception and semantic understanding but also directly affect latency-sensitive closed-loop robot action prediction. Existing schemes typically guide compression using redundancy or importance cues, such as visual similarity, attention scores, and saliency. However, these cues only indirectly measure the key factor for safe compression: how much a token can change before causing an unacceptable deviation in downstream actions. This receiver-dependent tolerance is closely related to the principle of just noticeable difference (JND). Classical JND characterizes signal tolerance in the human visual system, while machine-oriented JND extends this concept to downstream machine responses. Building on this progression, we introduce Action-JND, which extends JND modeling to embodied perception by defining noticeability through the language-conditioned action response of a vision-language-action (VLA) policy in closed-loop control. A token change is considered admissible only when the induced action deviation remains within a tolerated margin. To realize this concept, we develop a lightweight token-wise JND estimator in deep visual-feature space to predict the maximum tolerable perturbation while preserving policy responses. The resulting action-tolerance score serves as a plug-and-play criterion for VLA compression paradigms, including stale-KV reuse and token pruning, prioritizing action-tolerant tokens for compression. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark with OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT demonstrate that Action-JND consistently improves compression reliability, especially under aggressive compression ratios.

</details>


### [35] [Routing Before Looking: Query-Adaptive Evidence Acquisition for Long-form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20805)
*Tianyue Wang,Xuying Wu,Yuxiang Ma,Ruiming Liang,Jiaxuan Kang,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Leigang Qu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Route2Look是一个轻量级、模型无关的框架，通过路由策略动态选择证据获取工具（全局浏览、时间定位、语义检索），在长视频理解中实现查询自适应的证据获取，达到SOTA性能并保持高帧效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，现有方法（planning-before-perception）通常依赖单一策略（生成式或检索式），无法应对多样化的查询需求，导致查询需求与证据获取策略之间的不匹配。因此需要一种查询自适应的证据获取框架。

Method: Route2Look采用Route-Look-Memorize循环，包含三个工具：Global Browse（全局浏览）、Temporal Ground（时间定位）和Semantic Retrieve（语义检索）。核心是两阶段路由策略：首先通过生成式与检索式轨迹的差异对比分析来蒸馏路由技能，然后在推理阶段结合硬路由规则和继续/停止标准应用该技能。

Result: 在多个长视频基准上，Route2Look达到了SOTA性能，同时在不同数据集和查询类型上保持了强大的帧效率。Oracle路由分析进一步揭示了查询自适应证据获取的未来潜力。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的证据获取策略是长视频理解的关键发展方向，Route2Look通过路由策略统一了生成式与检索式方法的优势，为未来的长视频理解提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding remains challenging for video agents due to the mismatch between query demands and evidence acquisition strategies. Although recent planning-before-perception methods outperform query-agnostic pipelines, they often rely on a single dominant strategy, either generation-based strategy or retrieval-based strategy, limiting their ability to handle diverse query demands. We propose Route2Look, a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for query-adaptive evidence acquisition in long-form video understanding. Route2Look operates in a Route-Look-Memorize loop with three tools: Global Browse for holistic context, Temporal Ground for explicit temporal cues, and Semantic Retrieve for semantic search. The core component is a routing policy that dynamically selects evidence acquisition tools based on the query. To build this policy, Route2Look adopts a two-stage design: first distilling the routing skill from differential contrastive analysis between generation-based and retrieval-based trajectories, and then applying the distilled skill with hard routing rules and continue-or-stop criteria during inference. Experiments on challenging long-video benchmarks show that Route2Look achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining strong frame efficiency across datasets and query types. Oracle routing analysis further reveals the potential of query-adaptive evidence acquisition for future long-form video understanding.

</details>


### [36] [TRACE: Training-time Report-guided and Clinically Ordered Concept Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20809)
*Wentao Yue,Tianyou Lai,Jiayu Luo,Qingyu Mao,Ziying Wang,Zhenyuan Ning,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，利用训练时的结构化放射学报告作为特权概念监督，通过教师引导的概念编辑机制在恶性肿瘤感知的有序概念空间中细化图像派生概念；同时引入SCMT策略处理不完整标注问题，并通过编辑蒸馏训练图像-only自编辑器，实现测试阶段仅需图像的自主概念精炼。作者还发布了概念丰富的基准数据集BUSC。实验表明TRACE在多个数据集上性能优于现有方法，且跨域鲁棒性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺超声诊断依赖临床上有意义的语义概念，但大多数深度学习方法采用端到端图像到标签的范式，缺乏可解释性和鲁棒性。现有的基于概念的方法要么需要完整标注，要么在推理时要求多模态输入，严重限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，包含：(1) 利用结构化放射学报告作为特权概念监督；(2) 在恶性肿瘤感知的有序概念空间中通过教师引导编辑机制细化图像派生概念；(3) 提出SCMT策略应对不完整标注；(4) 通过编辑蒸馏训练图像-only自编辑器实现测试阶段自主概念精炼；(5) 构建概念丰富的BUSC基准数据集。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，TRACE相比现有方法取得了更优越的性能，并展现出更强的跨域鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TRACE有效解决了概念学习方法在乳腺超声诊断中面临的可解释性、标注不完整性和推理时多模态依赖等挑战，实现了测试阶段仅需图像的自主诊断，同时通过概念编辑机制提升了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound diagnosis relies on clinically meaningful semantic concepts, yet most deep learning methods adopt end-to-end image-to-label paradigms that lack interpretability and robustness. While concept-based approaches offer a promising alternative, they often assume complete annotations or require multimodal inputs at inference, which significantly limits their real-world applicability. To tackle these issues, we propose Training-time Report-guided and Clinically Ordered Concept Editing (TRACE), a training-time report-guided framework that leverages structured radiology reports as privileged concept supervision while enabling image-only diagnosis at test time. TRACE refines image-derived concepts through a teacher-guided editing mechanism within a malignancy-aware ordered concept space. To address incomplete annotations, we introduce Strategic Concept Missing Training (SCMT) and train an image-only self-editor via edit distillation for autonomous concept refinement. Besides, we introduce BUSC, a concept-enriched benchmark linking images, labels, and structured attributes. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that TRACE achieves superior performance and improved cross-domain robustness compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [37] [Enhancing Localized Reasoning for Long Video Understanding via Efficient Segment-to-Video Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20814)
*Beibei Zhang,Chao Xu,Jun Lan,Zongyi Li,Lai Wei,Huijia Zhu,Tongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Segment-to-Video (S2V) 的新型训练方法，通过基于局部片段生成VQA问答对，再将其迁移回整个视频上进行训练，以高效增强多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在长视频理解（LVU）中的细粒度推理能力。该方法仅需10K训练样本和使用简单准确率奖励的强化学习，即可在多个LVU基准上超越现有方法，同时在训练和推理效率上具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法存在两大问题：一是基于强化微调（RFT）的方法需要高昂的标注成本和复杂的奖励设计；二是自反思和迭代感知机制导致输出冗长、推理延迟高。作者希望在不引入额外推理开销的前提下，高效提升MLLMs在长视频中的细粒度推理能力。

Method: S2V方法的核心是：先在视频的局部片段上生成VQA问答对，再将基于片段的VQA迁移到整个视频上进行训练。由于片段级VQA聚焦于短时段，能自然捕捉到从全局视角容易忽视的细节，训练过程强迫MLLMs将细粒度细节与问答正确关联，从而避免全视频中的干扰噪声。训练仅使用简单准确率奖励的强化学习，基于10K VQA样本，推理时仅需单次前向传播且输出token有限。

Result: 实验结果表明，S2V在多个LVU基准上持续提升性能，不仅长视频理解准确率优于通用MLLMs和基于推理的方法，而且在训练和推理效率方面也更具优势。

Conclusion: S2V证明了通过片段到视频的监督迁移，可以在不增加推理成本和复杂训练开销的前提下，高效提升长视频理解中的细粒度推理能力，为LVU提供了一种兼具准确性和效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Though Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown impressive potential in video understanding, long video understanding (LVU) remains challenging since distracting noise in complex and lengthy contexts can obscure localized details, misleading MLLMs to produce incorrect answers. Recent works mitigate these issues by incentivizing deep reasoning to include relevant evidence. However, these methods have two main problems: First, the reinforcement fine-tuning framework (RFT) they leveraged incurs substantial training overheads, including high annotation costs and complicated reward designs. Second, the self-reflective and iterative-perception mechanism in some methods causes lengthy outputs and high inference latency. To alleviate these problems, we propose a novel Segment-to-Video Supervision} method (S2V) to efficiently enhance fine-grained reasoning in LVU. Specifically, we generate question answer pairs (VQA) based on localized segments, and then transfer these segment-based VQA back to the whole video for training. Due to focusing on short segments, segment-based VQA can naturally notice details which tend to be overlooked from a whole-video perspective. Training on such data can enforce MLLMs to correctly associate fine-grained details with QA while avoiding distracting noise in the whole video. The S2V training involves just reinforcement learning (RL) with a simple accuracy reward based on only 10K VQA samples and the resulting S2V model predicts answer using a single forward pass with limited output tokens. Experimental results demonstrate that S2V can consistently improve LVU performance across multiple LVU benchmarks, outperforming both general MLLMs and reasoning-based methods not only in LVU accuracy but also in training and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [38] [Identify, Locate, Link: End-to-End Key-Value Extraction from Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20868)
*A. Said Gurbuz,Ahmed Nassar,Christoph Auer,Maksym Lysak,Lucas Morin,Matteo Omenetti,Tim Strohmeyer,Panagiotis Vagenas,Nikolaos Livathinos,Michele Dolfi,Peter Staar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于256M参数轻量级视觉语言模型（SmolDocling）的端到端文档关键值提取方法，无需OCR预处理即可同时完成识别、定位和关联任务，并通过扩展DocTags标签、合成数据增强和布局感知评估框架，在FUNSD、XFUND及大规模私有数据集上超越更大规模的零样本VLM基线，模型体积小27倍且推理速度快5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档处理流水线将OCR引擎与下游结构化信息提取模型级联使用，导致多阶段错误传播问题。作者希望探索一种端到端的、无需OCR预处理的关键值提取方法，以消除级联系统中的错误累积。

Method: 1) 微调SmolDocling（256M参数VLM）实现端到端关键值提取，单次推理联合完成识别、定位和关联；2) 扩展DocTags，添加键、值、区域和链接特殊标签，支持多对多关系建模为统一输出序列；3) 设计数据增强流水线，结合合成表单填充和基于图的裁剪以保留完整关键值子图；4) 引入布局感知评估框架，在文本匹配基础上增加空间边界框验证。

Result: 在FUNSD、XFUND和大型私有数据集上，该模型在布局感知评估下优于更大的零样本VLM基线；相比Qwen2.5-VL（7B）模型，体积小27倍，推理速度快5倍以上。

Conclusion: 轻量级VLM采用端到端方法可以高效解决文档关键值提取任务，无需OCR预处理即可获得优于更大模型的性能，为文档智能处理提供了更高效、更经济的解决方案。模型权重将在发表后公开发布。

Abstract: Document processing pipelines traditionally cascade optical character recognition (OCR) engines with downstream models for structured information extraction, leading to multi-stage error propagation. We fine-tune SmolDocling, a compact 256M-parameter vision-language model (VLM), to perform end-to-end key-value extraction directly from document images, jointly solving identification, localization, and association in a single pass without OCR preprocessing. We extend DocTags with specialized key, value, region, and link tags, enabling many-to-many relationships in a unified output sequence. To address data limitations, we design an augmentation pipeline combining synthetic form filling and graph-based crops that preserve complete key-value subgraphs. We further introduce a layout-aware evaluation framework extending text matching with spatial bounding box verification. On FUNSD, XFUND, and a large-scale private dataset, our model outperforms larger zero-shot VLM baselines under layout-aware evaluation, while being 27 times smaller than Qwen2.5-VL (7B) and over 5 times faster at inference. The model weights will be released publicly after publication.

</details>


### [39] [RDANet: Relative Degradation Aware Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20870)
*Rui Liu,Jing Nie,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RDANet（相对退化感知网络）用于红外小目标检测，通过多尺度抗混叠下采样（MSAD）和原型引导跳跃记忆（PGSM）两个模块，分别解决目标结构在降采样中丢失和复杂背景下局部对比度不稳定两大问题，在三个公开数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测面临目标极小、弱对比度和复杂背景的挑战，现有检测器在目标尺度变化或背景变化时表现出不稳定的性能，主要原因是现有方法无法同时在下采样过程中保留目标结构，以及在背景变化下维持判别性局部对比度。

Method: 提出RDANet，包含两个核心模块：1) 多尺度抗混叠下采样（MSAD），通过多尺度抗混叠滤波结合像素折叠聚合，减少分辨率降低时的混叠效应，从而保留目标形状信息并抑制无关背景响应；2) 原型引导跳跃记忆（PGSM），从共享记忆中检索patch级原型并自适应融合到当前表示中，以在多样化场景背景下维持稳定的局部对比度线索。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集上，RDANet在大多数评估指标上取得了最佳性能；基于尺度和背景分层的评估表明，该方法在不同目标尺寸和场景复杂度下具有更稳定的检测行为。

Conclusion: RDANet通过针对性的双模块设计有效解决了红外小目标检测中的尺度敏感性和场景敏感性问题，提升了检测的鲁棒性和性能稳定性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection is still challenging in remote sensing imagery, because the targets are extremely small, exhibit weak local contrast, and are often embedded in complex and highly variable backgrounds. In addition to these inherent difficulties, we observe that existing detectors often show unstable performance when the target scale changes or when the scene background varies. This scale- and scene-sensitive degradation indicates that current methods are insufficient in simultaneously preserving target structure during feature downsampling and maintaining discriminative local contrast under background shifts, which finally results in unbalanced detection performance across different conditions. To improve detection robustness, this paper proposes a Relative Degradation Aware Network (RDANet) for infrared small target detection. RDANet consists of two dedicated modules: Multi-Scale Anti-Alias Downsampling (MSAD) and Prototype-Guided Skip Memory (PGSM). MSAD introduces multi-scale anti-alias filtering together with pixel-fold aggregation to reduce aliasing effects during resolution reduction, so that target shape information can be better preserved while irrelevant background responses are suppressed. PGSM further enhances the skip features by retrieving patch-level prototypes from a shared memory and adaptively integrating them into the current representation, which helps maintain stable local contrast cues under diverse scene backgrounds. Experiments on three public benchmarks show that RDANet achieves the best performance on most evaluation metrics, while scale- and background-stratified evaluations indicate more stable behavior across target sizes and scene complexity. The code is available at https://github.com/BIT-RuiLiu/RDANet.

</details>


### [40] [LoRC: Detecting AI-Generated Images via Low-Rank Collapse in Semantic Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20882)
*Haozhen Yan,Ruoxin Chen,Jiahui Zhan,Bo Wang,Youchang Xiao,Shouhong Ding,Liqing Zhang,Taiping Yao,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRC框架，从几何视角检测AI生成图像。研究发现现代生成器在语义残差正交子空间中存在低秩坍缩（秩退化），而在主导语义方向上表现正常。LoRC通过解耦语义主导性来捕获生成解码瓶颈导致的坍缩残差几何，在多个基准上平均提升7.0%准确率，在39个未见生成器上达到97.0%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成器能生成高度逼真的合成图像，宏观语义层面与真实图像难以区分，因此取证线索隐藏在微妙的非语义视觉差异中。现有方法未能有效揭示这种架构无关的深层签名，需要从新的视角（几何视角）寻找AI生成图像检测的可靠依据。

Method: 从几何视角回访AIGI检测，识别出架构无关的特征：现代生成器在语义残差正交子空间中表现出低秩坍缩（秩退化），同时保持主导语义方向。该结构扁平化在最终解码阶段一致出现，形成跨各种生成器架构的共享瓶颈。基于此签名，提出LoRC框架，解耦语义主导性以捕获生成解码瓶颈诱导的坍缩残差几何。

Result: LoRC在多个基准上平均提升7.0%准确率，在39个未见生成器上达到97.0%准确率，展现出强大的跨模型泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRC作为可靠的AIGI检测方法，在复杂真实环境中表现出强泛化性和鲁棒性，验证了生成器低秩坍缩这一架构无关签名作为检测依据的有效性。

Abstract: Modern generators faithfully model macroscopic semantics, producing synthetic images that appear highly realistic. Consequently, decisive forensic cues reside in subtle non-semantic visual discrepancies. To reveal these cues, we revisit AIGI detection from a geometric perspective and identify an architecture-agnostic signature. Specifically, modern generators exhibit low-rank collapse (\textit{i.e.}, rank degeneracy) in the semantic-residual orthogonal subspace while largely preserving the dominant semantic direction. This structural flattening consistently emerges during the final decoding stage, forming a shared bottleneck across diverse generator architectures. Motivated by this signature, we propose \textbf{LoRC}, a framework that decouples semantic dominance to capture the collapsed residual geometry induced by the generative decoding bottleneck. Our method improves accuracy by an average of 7.0\% across multiple benchmarks and achieves 97.0\% accuracy on 39 unseen generators. These results demonstrate strong cross-model generalization and robustness, making LoRC a reliable approach for AIGI detection in complex real-world environments.

</details>


### [41] [Breaking High Confidence: Practical Face Impersonation under High-Security Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20884)
*Changjin Kim,Seunghun Paik,Dongsoo Kim,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在高校验安全阈值设置下成功实现对人脸识别系统（FRS）的冒名攻击方法。在严格速率限制下，通过仅100次置信度分数查询，即可在LFW基准上对Amazon Rekognition实现超过92%的冒名成功率（阈值为99，适用于执法场景），并在多个开源FRS上验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统已广泛应用于银行、机场等关键认证场景，需要严格的安全配置。然而现有安全研究主要集中在中等安全阈值设置下，无法直接适用于高校验安全阈值的FRS场景。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索高校验阈值下FRS的脆弱性。

Method: 本文提出首个针对高校验安全阈值设置下FRS的冒名攻击方法。作者聚焦于严格速率限制下的基于分数（score-based）冒名攻击这一实际且富有挑战性的威胁模型，并提供了原理性的数学分析，刻画攻击流程各阶段存在的差距，从而精确评估攻击的可行性。

Result: 在LFW基准上，仅用100次置信度分数查询即可对Amazon Rekognition实现超过92%的冒名成功率（置信度阈值为99，即执法场景的推荐设置）。在多个开源FRS上，在类似严格决策阈值下均观察到一致且鲁棒的攻击性能。

Conclusion: 本研究证明即使在高安全阈值和严格速率限制的防御配置下，人脸识别系统仍然存在显著的冒名攻击风险。该工作为高校验阈值场景下FRS的安全评估提供了新的视角和基准，揭示了现有防御体系在严格安全设置下仍需加强。

Abstract: Face recognition systems (FRSs) are increasingly deployed in critical real-world services for authentication, such as banking applications and airport identity checks, necessitating stringent security configurations. Consequently, the security vulnerabilities of FRSs have garnered significant attention. While existing studies have extensively explored FRS security, prior analyses have primarily focused on medium-security threshold settings, which are not directly applicable to FRSs operating under high-security constraints. In this paper, we propose the first successful impersonation attack against FRSs under high-security threshold settings. Among various threat models, we focus on a practical and challenging scenario: score-based impersonation attacks under strict rate limits. To precisely evaluate the feasibility of such attacks, we provide a principled mathematical analysis characterizing the gaps in each stage of the attack pipeline. Our method significantly enhances impersonation capabilities in score-based attacks, even under elevated decision thresholds. On the LFW benchmark, with a budget of only 100 confidence score queries per identity, our attack achieves an impersonation success rate exceeding 92\% against Amazon Rekognition at a confidence score threshold of 99-recommended setting for law enforcement scenarios. We further observe consistently robust performance across multiple open-source FRSs evaluated at similarly stringent decision thresholds.

</details>


### [42] [EviRank: Structured Relevance Evidence for Multimodal Image Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20886)
*Enjun Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Xinyu Zuo,Jinwen Luo,Ruiwen Tao,Lisheng Duan,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Junfu Pu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出EviRank，将多模态图像检索重排序重新定义为语义约束满足问题。系统将任意查询（纯文本、纯图像或组合查询）解析为统一的证据包，涵盖六个语义槽位（实体、属性、关系等），并标注为必需、禁止或可忽略。重排序过程通过确定性评分和基于证据的列表比较实现，全程无需训练。在五个基准测试上达到SOTA，蒸馏的学生模型保留教师90%以上的能力且成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界图像检索查询是多模态且组合性的（如"找这件衬衫的粉色版本"），需要同时保留实体、修改属性和忽略上下文。然而现有的重排序器要么将多面相关性压缩为不透明的嵌入，要么依赖自由形式的思维链，容易遗漏或幻觉细粒度约束。这促使作者将NLP中基于评分标准和检查表的评估方法引入图像重排序。

Method: EviRank将多模态图像重排序视为语义约束满足问题。首先将任意查询（文本、图像或组合）解析为统一的证据包，包含六个语义槽位的类型化标准（实体、属性、关系等），每个标注为必需、禁止或可忽略。重排序过程采用证据条件验证，结合确定性评分标准和基于证据的列表比较，全程无需训练。此外，显式证据可作为结构化监督信号，用于可选地蒸馏轻量级学生模型。

Result: 在涵盖文本到图像、图像到图像和组合图像检索的五个基准测试上，EviRank取得了最先进的性能。蒸馏后的学生模型保留了教师模型90%以上的能力，同时计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: EviRank通过将复杂的多模态查询显式分解为可验证的语义约束，提供了一种可解释、训练自由且高效的图像重排序方法。其显式证据机制不仅提升了性能，还实现了向轻量级模型的高效知识蒸馏，为多模态检索提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Real-world image search queries are multimodal and compositional: ``find this shirt in pink'' specifies an entity to retain, an attribute to modify, and context to ignore. Yet existing re-rankers either compress such multifaceted relevance into an opaque embedding or rely on free-form chain-of-thought that easily omits or hallucinates fine-grained constraints. Drawing on rubric- and checklist-based evaluation from NLP, we recast multimodal image re-ranking as a semantic constraint satisfaction problem and propose EviRank, which parses any query - text-only, image-only, or composed - into a unified evidence package: typed criteria across six semantic slots (e.g., entities, attributes, relations), each labelled required, forbidden, or ignorable. Re-ranking then reduces to evidence-conditioned verification, combining deterministic rubric scoring and evidence-grounded listwise comparison in a single training-free procedure. The explicit evidence can further serve as structured supervision for optionally distilling a lightweight student. Across five benchmarks spanning text-to-image, image-to-image, and composed image retrieval, EviRank achieves state-of-the-art performance, and the distilled student preserves over 90% of the teacher's capability at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [43] [EmotionDialogCN: A Spontaneous Multimodal Dataset for Mandarin Emotional Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20905)
*Yi Zheng,Yifan Xu,Yan Zhou,Hejia Chen,Chunyu Qiang,Xiaoqiang Liu,Xiaohan Li,Shenze Huang,Yue Zhang,Guoying Zhao,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmotionDialogCN，一个大规模视听情感对话数据集，包含21,880个对话片段、119位专业演员、20个日常场景、18种情感类别和超过400小时录音，通过新型数据采集框架减少设备干扰，情感分布与真实人类统计偏差仅0.64（远优于先前数据集的5.65），并验证了其在多模态情感分析任务中的稳定表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态对话数据集在情感标注质量、情感多样性和数据规模方面存在明显不足，难以支持真实面对面视听交互中的情感理解研究。

Method: 构建EmotionDialogCN数据集：由119位专业演员在20个日常场景中表演21,880个对话会话，覆盖18种情感类别，总时长超过400小时；采用新型数据采集框架最小化设备干扰，使情感表达更加自然细腻。

Result: EmotionDialogCN的情感分布偏差仅为0.64，远优于先前数据集的5.65；主体取景一致（52-59%帧占比）；在声学、词汇和视觉模态上均展现出稳定的单模态和多模态性能，融合结果进一步证明多模态对齐和跨模态互补性。

Conclusion: EmotionDialogCN是目前规模最大、最全面的视听情感对话数据集，其真实的情感分布和高数据质量能够有效支撑多模态情感分析研究，促进跨模态互补与融合。

Abstract: Face-to-face audiovisual interaction is central to human communication, conveying rich emotional and social cues. However, existing multimodal dialogue datasets remain limited by inadequate emotion annotations, poor emotional diversity, and small scale. We introduce EmotionDialogCN, a large-scale audiovisual-emotional dataset designed to capture authentic face-to-face communication. It contains 21,880 dialogue sessions performed by 119 professional actors across 20 everyday scenarios, covering 18 emotion categories with over 400 hours of recordings, the largest and most comprehensive dataset of its kind. A novel data collection framework minimizes equipment interference, enabling natural and nuanced emotional expressions. EmotionDialogCN achieves an emotion distribution deviation of 0.64 from real human emotion statistics (versus 5.65 for prior datasets) and consistent subject framing (52-59% frame occupancy). Together, these properties translate into stable unimodal and multimodal performance across acoustic, lexical, and visual modalities, with fusion results further underscoring strong multimodal alignment and cross-modal complementarity.

</details>


### [44] [InfinityEdit: Infinite Video Editing with a Lightweight Edit-Ignition Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20910)
*Yunze Tong,Mushui Liu,Canyu Zhao,Shiyi Zhang,Didi Zhu,Peng Zhang,Wanggui He,Jinlong Liu,Ying Chen,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无限视频编辑任务：给定前序片段和编辑指令，模型需生成延续流并应用编辑的下一个片段，该过程可重复无限次。作者设计了数据收集流程并提出InfinityEdit轻量级编辑适配器，包含历史交叉注意力、时序因果自注意力和编辑交叉注意力三个模块，仅在编辑指令到达的分块中激活，保持原始模型无限生成能力。实验证明其在连续编辑下保持流式延续和生成稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的视频编辑方法大多依赖原地编辑假设，即把编辑后的视频与源片段在固定时间跨度内逐帧对齐，无法应对开放式流（如直播游戏重风格化或对持续镜头施加相机运动），编辑需要扩展到未来帧而非应用于静态输入片段。

Method: 首先设计面向无限视频编辑的数据收集流程；基于此提出InfinityEdit轻量级编辑适配器，包含三个注意力模块：历史交叉注意力（用输入帧引导去噪帧）、时序因果自注意力（时序线索仅从早帧流向晚帧）、编辑交叉注意力（注入编辑请求）。推理时适配器仅在编辑指令到达的分块中激活，后续分块由原始模型配合重置锚帧生成。

Result: 大量实验表明InfinityEdit在每次编辑下都能忠实延续流，且在无界编辑序列上保持稳定，同时保留原始模型的无限生成能力。

Conclusion: 无限视频编辑是一个值得研究的新范式，InfinityEdit通过轻量级适配器和按需激活机制有效解决了编辑延续性和累积稳定性两大挑战。

Abstract: With large pretrained models, existing methods have effectively improved instruction-based video editing. However, most of them rely on an in-place editing assumption. They align the edited video with the given source clip frame by frame over a fixed time span. This pattern fails for open-ended streams, e.g., restyling a live game or applying a camera move to an ongoing shot. In such cases, edits must extend to future frames as they arrive, rather than be applied to a static input clip. In this paper, we study this setting and name it infinite video editing: given a preceding segment and an edit request, a model must generate the next segment that continues the stream while applying the requested edit. This process repeats as an unbounded sequence of edit instructions arrives. This task brings two challenges: the edit must be a faithful continuation rather than a frame-wise rewrite, and generation quality must remain stable as edits accumulate. To address them, we first design a data-collection pipeline for infinite video editing. Based on the collected data, we propose InfinityEdit, a lightweight edit adapter that equips a streaming video generator with unbounded editing ability. The adapter contains three attention modules. History cross-attention guides the denoising frames using the input frames. Temporal causal self-attention keeps temporal cues flowing only from earlier frames to later ones. Edit cross-attention injects the edit request into generation. During inference, the adapter is activated only in the chunk where an edit request arrives. Subsequent chunks are generated by the original model with a reset anchor frame. This scheme applies the edit while preserving the original model's infinite generation ability. Extensive experiments show that InfinityEdit faithfully continues the stream under each edit, and stays stable over unbounded edit sequences.

</details>


### [45] [Explainable Deepfake Detection with Feature-robust Augmentation and Evidence-grounded Explanation Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20913)
*Zhu Xu,Jiaqi Tang,Pokai Chen,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释深度伪造检测框架，包含两个核心创新：特征鲁棒增强（FRA）通过多样化退化感知增强策略、监督对比学习与均值教师架构来应对低质量图像的检测精度下降问题；证据引导的偏好优化（EPO）通过学习"接受-拒绝"解释对来确保模型生成基于真实操纵痕迹的事实性解释。该方法在ACM Multimedia 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释深度伪造检测方法存在两大缺陷：(1)检测模型对图像质量退化敏感，在低质量样本上精度骤降，而简单数据增强策略可能引入特征漂移；(2)解释模型可能遗漏操纵证据或产生与事实不符的幻觉细节，削弱可解释性。

Method: 框架包含两项创新：(1)特征鲁棒增强：采用多样化退化感知增强策略，结合监督对比学习和均值教师架构，通过一致性约束稳定特征以应对增强变换；(2)证据引导的偏好优化：通过构造"接受-拒绝"解释对（拒绝样本通过证据遗漏或无关信息注入构建），引导模型优先关注真实的操纵痕迹。

Result: 该方法的有效性通过在ACM Multimedia 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛中荣获第一名得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过特征鲁棒增强与证据引导偏好优化的双重创新，本文同时解决了深度伪造检测在低质量图像下的鲁棒性问题以及解释事实性不足的问题，为可解释深度伪造检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Explainable deepfake detection extends binary classification by requiring models to not only predict authenticity but also provide interpretable justifications. This expanded scope is critical in practice, where users like forensic analysts need insight into the rationale behind the detection. Despite advancements, current approaches suffer from two critical deficiencies: (1)vulnerability to image quality degradation: detection accuracy plummets on low-quality samples, while naive augmentation strategies may induce feature drift and impair performance as diversity expands. (2) factually flawed explanations: explanation models may omit manipulation evidence or hallucinate irrelevant details, undermining interpretability. To address it, we propose a framework with two innovations. For robust deepfake detection, we introduce Feature-robust Augmentation, which comprises diversified degradation-aware augmentation strategies, and a supervised contrastive learning pattern paired with a mean-teacher architecture that stabilizes features against augmentations through consistency constraints. For explanation, we devise an evidence-grounded preference optimization process that guides model to prioritize genuine manipulation traces by learning from chosen-rejected explanation pairs, where rejected samples are constructed via evidence omission or irrelevant information injection. The proposed approach wins the first place in ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge.The code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/EDD.git.

</details>

*Qifeng Zhang,Ting Xiang,Zeyuan Bai,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义定位增强教师（SLE-T）框架，通过轻量级SLE Adapter将局部纹理先验注入DINOv2，解决VFM在域自适应目标检测中教师与学生特征图的空间尺度不匹配和伪标签质量问题，在三个基准上达到SOTA性能，且用小模型实现了接近大模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VFM的域自适应目标检测方法忽视了教师和学生特征图之间的空间尺度差异，导致语义不兼容，削弱了特征对齐和伪标签学习；同时，域偏移会导致源域训练的VFM教师漏检目标域物体，伪标签质量受限。

Method: 提出SLE-T框架：1）设计轻量级SLE Adapter，将预训练的局部纹理先验注入DINOv2以提升跨域识别能力；2）将DINOv2特征重构为与学生检测器空间和语义兼容的稠密表示；3）通过伪标签学习或特征对齐两种方式传递教师知识。

Result: 在三个DAOD基准上达到SOTA性能；消融实验证实教师-学生语义兼容性的重要性；SLE-T配合DINOv2-B可产生与DINOv2-G相当或更优的伪标签，仅需其约四分之一的训练时间和更少的GPU内存。

Conclusion: SLE-T通过解决VFM教师与学生之间的语义兼容性问题，实现了高效且高质量的知识迁移，在有限计算资源下也能获得优异的域自适应目标检测性能。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) offer strong generalization capabilities for domain-adaptive object detection (DAOD). However, existing VFM-based methods overlook the spatial-scale discrepancy between teacher and student feature maps, resulting in semantic incompatibility that weakens both feature alignment and pseudo-label learning. Moreover, domain shift can cause source-trained VFM teachers to miss target-domain objects, limiting the quality of their pseudo-labels. To address these issues, we propose the Semantic Localization-Enhanced Teacher (SLE-T), a semantically compatible knowledge-distillation framework built around a lightweight SLE Adapter for DINOv2. SLE Adapter injects pretrained local-texture priors into DINOv2 to improve cross-domain recognition and reformulates its features into dense representations that are spatially and semantically compatible with the student detector. SLE-T transfers the resulting teacher knowledge through either pseudo-label learning or feature alignment. We instantiate SLE-T with DINOv2-B and DINOv2-L (the ViT-B and ViT-L variants) and compare them with the larger DINOv2-G teacher. Extensive experiments on three DAOD benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, and ablation studies confirm the importance of teacher-student semantic compatibility. Notably, SLE-T with DINOv2-B produces competitive or superior pseudo-labels using approximately one-quarter of the training time of DINOv2-G and substantially less GPU memory, demonstrating efficient VFM knowledge transfer under limited computational resources.

</details>


### [47] [GAP-SAM: A Global Artifact Prior for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20929)
*Haozhen Yan,Siyuan Shan,Zijian Yu,Youqi Wang,Yan Hong,Jun Lan,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP-SAM，通过将图像与其冻结VAE重建编码为全局伪影令牌，并注入SAM3的特征金字塔（零门控FiLM）来提升AI生成图像篡改定位的域外（OOD）性能。在六个数据集上平均Pixel-F1达79.8，超越最强基线12.6个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像篡改定位的OOD性能滞后于图像级检测，原因是像素级监督将取证证据与数据集特有的掩码几何和语义边界纠缠在一起。作者发现COCO-ControlNet通过对齐语义和几何可提升OOD性能，但VAE重建对齐效果不佳，且存在"边界粘附"问题——分割模型倾向于预测语义轮廓而非真实篡改边界。

Method: 作者首先构建COCO-ControlNet（利用Canny边缘和深度图对齐语义与几何）并测试Mask-VAE重建对齐方法。基于发现（VAE伪影迁移差、边界粘附问题），提出GAP-SAM：将输入图像及其冻结VAE重建编码为全局伪影令牌，通过零门控FiLM注入SAM3特征金字塔，在像素解码前调制密集解码，无需预设空间区域即可保留定位能力并抑制语义边界捷径。

Result: 在六个数据集上，GAP-SAM平均Pixel-F1为79.8，比最强先前方法高出12.6个点；并且在JPEG压缩、高斯模糊和缩放等所有测试的退化严重程度下均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 全局伪影令牌（而非空间区域预设）结合零门控特征调制，是提升篡改定位OOD鲁棒性的有效策略，能够抑制语义边界捷径并保留精确定位能力。

Abstract: AI-generated image manipulation localization identifies edited pixels, but its OOD performance lags behind image-level detection partly because pixel supervision entangles forensic evidence with dataset-specific mask geometry and semantic boundaries. Extending image-level distribution alignment to localization, we construct COCO-ControlNet with source-image Canny edges and depth maps to align semantics and geometry, improving OOD performance across multiple localizers. Yet tighter Mask-VAE Reconstruction Alignment (Mask-VAE) underperforms COCO-ControlNet, showing that VAE reconstruction artifacts transfer poorly to local diffusion-inpainting artifacts. We also identify \emph{boundary adhesion}, where fine-tuned segmentation models snap predictions to semantic object contours rather than true manipulation boundaries. These findings motivate GAP-SAM, which encodes an image and its frozen VAE reconstruction into a global artifact token and injects it into SAM3's feature pyramid via zero-gated FiLM before pixel decoding. Without prescribing a spatial region, this token modulates dense decoding to preserve localization while suppressing semantic-boundary shortcuts. Across six datasets, GAP-SAM averages 79.8 Pixel-F1, outperforming the strongest prior method by 12.6 points. It also performs best at every tested severity of JPEG compression, Gaussian blur, and resizing.

</details>


### [48] [OccluRank: Controllable Occlusion-Aware Layout-to-Image Generation by Adding Just an Ordinal Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20932)
*Wenyang Hong,Yuan Wang,Yanbin Hao,Lanqing Xue,Ke Wang,Xiang Wang,Kuien Liu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OccluRank，一种简单且可控的遮挡感知布局到图像生成框架，仅需为每个边界框添加一个序数排名即可编码遮挡顺序，并通过Order-aware Instance Interaction模块实现遮挡感知的实例交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布局到图像生成中边界框只能表示实例位置，无法表达实例间的遮挡顺序。现有方法要么依赖额外的几何条件，要么采用复杂的推理流程，要么独立构建实例表示而不显式建模遮挡相关的交互关系，缺乏简单有效的遮挡顺序建模方案。

Method: OccluRank通过两个核心组件实现：(1) 基于秩的轻量条件编码来编码用户指定的遮挡顺序；(2) Order-aware Instance Interaction (OII)模块在聚合前联合更新秩条件化的实例表示，使指定顺序能指导遮挡实例间的信息交换。此外构建了OccluLayout合成训练数据集，其遮挡顺序和amodal标注直接从已知场景几何导出；并提出OccluLayout-Bench评估基准，使用多个多模态大语言模型评估器。

Result: 实验表明OccluRank在保持属性一致性和整体图像质量的同时，能更可靠地保留目标实例、遵循指定布局并实现期望的遮挡关系。

Conclusion: OccluRank验证了一个简单的秩标注方案足以实现有效的遮挡感知布局到图像生成，无需额外的几何输入或推理时优化，为遮挡控制提供了轻量且可控的解决方案。

Abstract: Layout-to-image generation enables explicit spatial control through bounding-box layouts, yet bounding boxes specify only instance locations and cannot represent their occlusion order. Existing methods may rely on additional geometric conditions, employ complex inference procedures, or aggregate independently constructed instance representations without explicitly modeling their occlusion-dependent interactions. We propose OccluRank, a simple and controllable occlusion-aware layout-to-image framework that augments each bounding box with only one ordinal rank. OccluRank encodes the user-specified occlusion order through lightweight rank-based conditioning and introduces an Order-aware Instance Interaction (OII) module to jointly update rank-conditioned instance representations before aggregation. This allows the specified order to guide information exchange among occluding instances without additional geometric inputs or specialized inference-time optimization. We further construct OccluLayout, a synthetic training dataset whose occlusion order and amodal annotations are derived directly from known scene geometry rather than estimated from partially occluded images using auxiliary prediction models. For comprehensive evaluation, we introduce OccluLayout-Bench, which uses multiple multimodal large language model evaluators to assess instance presence, spatial layout, attributes, and occlusion order, together with FID for overall image quality. Experiments show that OccluRank more reliably preserves target instances, follows specified layouts, and realizes desired occlusion relationships while maintaining comparable attribute consistency and overall image quality.

</details>


### [49] [LHMCF-Net: A Learned Hyperbolic Mean Curvature Flow Network for Medical Images Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20942)
*Shuangshuang Duan,Chunlei He,Shoujun Huang,Dexing Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LHMCF-Net，一种基于学习型双曲平均曲率流的深度展开分割网络，通过将二阶耗散双曲PDE的数值迭代过程映射为网络离散演化阶段，实现了特征空间数据保真与深度结构先验的统一，在低对比度和模糊边界的医学图像分割任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Chan-Vese模型受限于一阶抛物型流的局限性，容易陷入噪声诱导的局部极小值，且难以在低对比度或模糊区域中保持轮廓传播的连贯性。作者旨在利用深度先验捕获复杂空间结构的能力，结合双曲型PDE的惯性机制来克服这些挑战。

Method: 提出LHMCF模型，由二阶耗散双曲PDE控制，通过引入速度场为演化界面提供惯性和动量。构建深度展开网络LHMCF-Net，将连续PDE的数值迭代过程映射为一系列离散演化阶段，每个阶段对应底层动力系统的一个物理解释更新，支持端到端优化并保留PDE的稳定性和几何一致性。

Result: 在三个公开医学分割数据集上的综合实验表明，LHMCF-Net在低对比度和边界不清的挑战性场景中取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 将双曲几何演化嵌入深度展开架构是有效的，物理启发的模型为鲁棒的医学图像分割提供了新的范式，展示了在统一高维框架中融合数据保真与深度结构先验的潜力。

Abstract: Motivated by the classical Chan-Vese model and the ability of deep priors to capture complex spatial structures, we develop a segmentation model that leverages learned hyperbolic mean curvature flow (LHMCF) as a mathematical foundation for integrating feature space data fidelity and deep structural priors within a unified high-dimensional framework. The proposed LHMCF model is governed by a second-order dissipative hyperbolic PDE, where the introduction of a velocity field provides inertia and momentum to the evolving interface. This hyperbolic mechanism enables the contour to bypass noise-induced local minima and propagate coherently through low-contrast or ambiguous regions, addressing limitations inherent to first-order parabolic flows. To solve the continuous LHMCF model, we construct a deep unfolding network, named LHMCF-Net, which maps the iterative numerical procedure of the PDE into a sequence of discrete evolution stages. Each stage corresponds to one physically interpretable update of the underlying dynamical system, allowing the network to inherit the stability and geometric consistency of the PDE while supporting end-to-end optimization. Comprehensive experiments on three publicly available medical segmentation datasets demonstrate that LHMCF-Net achieves superior performance, particularly in challenging scenarios with low contrast and unclear boundaries. These results highlight the effectiveness of embedding hyperbolic geometric evolution into deep unfolding architectures and underscore the potential of physically inspired models for robust medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [50] [SuppreSensing: Expert-Guided Feature Recalibration and Discrepancy Augmentation for Multimodal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20944)
*Xin Wu,Zhenyu Gao,Qiankun Zhang,Shaoyong Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SuppreSensing，一种将多模态融合重构为选择性协作过程的遥感多模态目标检测方法，包含EMFR模块和ECFP模块，在DroneVehicle和VEDAI数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感多模态目标检测面临语义异质性和模态特定噪声干扰的挑战，现有融合方法存在对称陷阱和跨模态差异处理不足的问题。

Method: SuppreSensing包含两个核心模块：(1) Expert-driven Multimodal Feature Recalibration (EMFR)模块，将共享共识提取重构为输入自适应的多专家选择过程，缓解多模态融合中的对称陷阱；(2) modality-specific属性增强策略，通过建模双向差异模式增强特定模态特征；(3) Expert-driven Customized Feature Purification (ECFP)模块，基于"专项检查-综合分析-诊断更新"的体检范式，迭代过滤冗余并强化任务相关语义。

Result: 在DroneVehicle和VEDAI数据集上取得最先进的检测性能；在FLIR和LLVIP自然场景数据集上的跨域评估验证了其在不同环境条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SuppreSensing通过选择性协作的融合框架有效解决了多模态融合中的语义异质性和噪声干扰问题，同时具备良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal object detection in remote sensing faces challenges due to semantic heterogeneity and modality-specific noise interference. To this end, we propose SuppreSensing, which reformulates multimodal fusion as a selective collaboration process that jointly models shared information and modality-specific cues. SuppreSensing first designs an Expert-driven Multimodal Feature Recalibration (EMFR) module, which reformulates shared-consensus extraction as an input-adaptive multi-expert selection process to alleviate the symmetry trap in multimodal fusion. Complementing this, a modality-specific attribute augmentation strategy is employed to enhance specific modality features by modeling bidirectional discrepancy patterns, mitigating cross-modal heterogeneity. Furthermore, we propose an Expert-driven Customized Feature Purification (ECFP) module based on a "specialized inspection-comprehensive analysis-diagnostic update" physical examination paradigm to iteratively filter redundancies and reinforce task-relevant semantics. Extensive experiments on the DroneVehicle and VEDAI datasets demonstrate that SuppreSensing achieves state-of-the-art detection performance. Cross-domain evaluations on natural scene datasets (FLIR and LLVIP) further validate its superior robustness and generalization capability across diverse environmental conditions.

</details>


### [51] [Kinematic Knowledge Maps for Pattern Alignment: Structured Latent Representational Learning in Multimodal Gait Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20969)
*Chen Dong,He Zonglin,Cheung Kenneth M. C*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScoliDetect，一个基于运动学知识图谱（KKM）的可解释框架，用于从单目步态视频中筛查青少年特发性脊柱侧弯。通过视频、KKM和模板化运动学文本的三模态交叉注意力融合，在1,858例多中心队列中实现ROC-AUC 0.972，并提供因子级可验证解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床AI面临两个挑战：弱对齐的输入模态以及缺乏领域特定的可解释表示。尤其在密集视频流、结构化时间序列和基于模板的运动学文本之间，仅靠传统融合方式难以实现有效的跨模态学习与临床可解释性。

Method: 构建运动学知识图谱（KKM），这是一种固定索引的结构化表示，编码绝对运动、自骨架配置和关节间信号相关性三方面的步态特征。将视频、KKM和模板化运动学文本通过双向交叉注意力与潜在瓶颈聚合进行三模态融合。采用分阶段训练协议，在架构选择后进行三模态对比预训练作为表示初始化。

Result: 在1,858例多中心队列中，KKM介导的多模态融合优于单模态模型和晚期拼接方法。三模态对比预训练将外部筛选队列的ROC-AUC从0.961提升至0.972。KKM的结构化特性提供了映射到特定运动学阶段和骨骼索引的因子级属性归因。

Conclusion: 将显式结构拓扑嵌入潜在空间可显著提升多模态模式分析系统的泛化能力和可解释性，为临床AI的可靠应用提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical AI is limited by weakly aligned inputs and the absence of domain-specific interpretable representations, particularly when learning from dense video stream, structured time-series, and template-based kinematic text. Here we present ScoliDetect, an explainable framework for adolescent idiopathic scoliosis screening from monocular gait video, built around a kinematic knowledge map (KKM) and complementary template-based kinematic text derived from per-sequence pose statics. KKM is a fixed-index structured representation that encodes gait features across absolute motion, self-skeleton configuration and joint-joint signal correlation, providing anchor-referenced multimodal fusion and factor-level interpretation. We integrate video, KKM, and template-based kinematic text through bidirectional cross-attention with latent-bottleneck aggregation. In a multicenter cohort (n = 1,858 after exclusions), prespecified supervised ablations on an external screening cohort show that KKM-mediated multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal models and late concatenation. Under a staged training protocol, trimodal contrastive pretraining is applied after architecture selection as representation initialization, improving external ROC-AUC from 0.961 to 0.972. Furthermore, the structured nature of the KKM provides inherent, factor-level attributions mapped directly to specific kinematic phases and skeletal indices, offering verifiable interpretability. The results demonstrate that embedding explicit structural topologies into latent spaces significantly enhances both the generalization and explainability of multimodal pattern analysis systems.

</details>


### [52] [WA-JEPA: Rethinking the Video JEPA Paradigm for World-Action Modeling in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20974)
*Xinlin Wang,Yujiao Xiang,Yuheng Zhou,Jingqi Wang,Minqing Huang,Jiajie Huang,Dongxu Wei,Tingguang Zhou,Xiyang Wang,Gong Chen,Zhi Xu,Feiyang Tan,Hangning Zhou,Mu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WA-JEPA，一个基于V-JEPA原生范式的世界-动作联合模型，用于自动驾驶规划。通过混合未来掩码预训练、条件流匹配生成未来潜在特征，以及联合未来-动作预测器，在NAVSIM-v2和HUGSIM基准上超越现有最强的端到端和世界-动作基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V-JEPA虽然能学习强大的时空表征，但其基于随机掩码补全和确定性回归的设计，无法满足自动驾驶规划中对未来导向预测和动作紧密结合的需求。

Method: (1) 混合未来掩码预训练：从观测上下文推断未来潜在特征，替代随机时空掩码；(2) 将确定性回归重构为潜在未来上的条件流匹配，提升未来潜在特征生成的合理性；(3) 提出联合未来-动作预测器，在统一的时空潜在空间中同时去噪未来场景token和自车轨迹，使动作监督直接塑造规划相关的世界表征。

Result: 在naplan视频上预训练并在NAVSIM上微调，WA-JEPA在NAVSIM-v2上达到91.7 EPDMS，超越最强端到端基线1.6、世界-动作基线1.3；在无需HUGSIM特定微调的情况下，在闭环HUGSIM基准上取得最优HD-Score 0.4462。

Conclusion: 验证了V-JEPA原生世界-动作建模是自动驾驶规划中强大且可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Video Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) learns powerful spatiotemporal representations from video through self-supervised latent feature prediction. However, V-JEPA is built around random-mask completion and deterministic regression, making it fundamentally ill-suited for autonomous driving planning that demands future-directed prediction tightly coupled with action. To address this, we rethink the V-JEPA paradigm and present WA-JEPA, a V-JEPA-native world-action model designed for autonomous driving planning. Instead of random spatiotemporal masking, WA-JEPA employs hybrid future-masked pre-training, where the model infers future latents from observed context. Departing from deterministic regression, we recast future prediction as conditional flow matching over latent futures, which substantially improves the model's ability to generate plausible future latents for downstream planning. Finally, a joint future-action predictor is proposed to denoise future scene tokens and ego trajectories together in a unified spatiotemporal latent space, allowing action supervision to directly shape planning-relevant world representations. Pre-trained on nuPlan videos and fine-tuned on NAVSIM, WA-JEPA reaches 91.7 EPDMS on NAVSIM-v2, surpassing the strongest end-to-end and world-action baselines by 1.6 and 1.3 EPDMS, and, without HUGSIM-specific fine-tuning, attains the best HD-Score of 0.4462 on the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark under the same evaluation protocol. These results validate V-JEPA-native world-action modeling as a powerful and scalable paradigm for autonomous driving planning. Code is available at https://github.com/AFARI-Research/WA-JEPA.

</details>


### [53] [MigrationNarrate: A Dataset for Detection of Migration Narratives in YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20984)
*Fatima Haouari,Carolina Scarton,Kalina Bontcheva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MigrationNarrate，首个用于英国移民叙事检测的多模态数据集，包含1,115个YouTube视频转录文本，采用两级分类体系（12个超级叙事和53个叙事标签），并提供了基于预训练编码器模型和开源/闭源大语言模型的基准测试结果及错误分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事对于理解社会传播至关重要，但移民叙事研究因缺乏标注数据集而严重不足；同时公众传播正转向视频平台，多模态信号中的叙事检测尚未被充分探索，这两大空白亟需填补。

Method: 构建英国移民语境下的多模态数据集MigrationNarrate，收集1,115个YouTube视频转录文本，设计包含12个移民超级叙事和53个叙事标签的两级分类体系，并利用预训练编码器模型以及开源和闭源大语言模型进行基准测试。

Result: 完成了数据集的构建与标注，提供了多种模型的基准测试结果，并通过深入的错误分析揭示了当前模型在移民叙事检测中的局限性与改进方向。

Conclusion: MigrationNarrate填补了移民叙事多模态检测领域的空白，为后续研究者提供了高质量数据集和基准参考，错误分析为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Narratives are central to how social communication is framed, making their detection critical for understanding and analysing public discourse. Prior work has explored narrative detection and extraction across diverse domains; however, migration narratives remain significantly understudied, primarily due to the absence of dedicated annotated datasets. Furthermore, public communication has recently shifted towards video-centric platforms, where narratives are conveyed through multimodal signals and consumed at scale. Despite this shift, narratives in videos remain largely unexplored. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MigrationNarrate, the first multimodal dataset for detection of migration narratives in the UK, consisting of 1,115 YouTube video transcripts annotated using a two-level taxonomy of 12 migration super-narratives and 53 narrative labels. This paper details the dataset design, collection, and annotations; together with benchmark results using a combination of pre-trained encoder models and both open- and closed-source Large Language Models. Finally, a thorough error analysis offers insights for future work.

</details>


### [54] [Latent Ordinal Evidence, Misaligned Outputs: Inference-Time Ordinal Lens Alignment for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20999)
*Haiming Li,Yingsheng Liu,Jingmin Zhu,Siyuan Yan,Xieji Li,Jiajun Sun,Zhen Yu,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在视觉序数回归任务（如年龄估计、图像质量评估、疾病分级）中的可靠性问题。发现隐状态中序数信息可线性恢复（Spearman相关系数高达0.938），但原生数字token输出仅暴露<1.15%的序数方向信息，导致16到77个绝对点的精度差距。作者提出Ordinal Lens Alignment（OLA）方法，通过冻结骨干网络并在解码器中间层训练轻量透镜，融合成序数分布并在生成时仅校正数字token logits，在多数设置下超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在视觉序数回归任务中需要将内部序数证据转化为有序数字token输出，但作者发现原生输出并不能可靠地暴露内部序数信息，存在"隐状态有序但输出无序"的显著差距，因此希望探究这一不一致性并设计改进方法。

Method: 作者首先通过线性探针在隐藏状态上验证序数信息的可恢复性，并分析unembedding矩阵对序数方向的过滤效应。随后提出OLA方法：冻结MLLM骨干，在解码器的中深层训练轻量的W_S锚定透镜，将多透镜输出融合为序数分布，并在生成阶段仅校正数字token的logits。

Result: 在4个序数基准和4个MLLM骨干上验证：隐状态序数线性可恢复性达到Spearman 0.938，而数字token行空间仅保留<1.15%序数信息，原生与线性探针输出间存在16-77绝对点精度差距。OLA在大多数设置中超越LoRA微调的OrderChain基线，在大部分单元中胜过判别式序数基线，并在所有设置下优于离线透镜方法。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了MLLM序数回归中"隐状态有序、输出无序"的根本性不匹配问题，并证明无需微调骨干即可通过轻量推断时透镜（OLA）有效纠正数字token输出的序数偏差，为MLLM序数任务提供了高效且冻结模型的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs apply the language model interface to visual inputs, where ordinal regression tasks such as age estimation, image quality assessment, and disease grading require autoregressive decisions over ordered class labels. We ask whether MLLMs reliably convert internal ordinal evidence into ordered digit-token outputs. Across four ordinal benchmarks and four MLLM backbones, ordinal labels are linearly recoverable from hidden states with Spearman correlation up to 0.938, and a task-designed prompt further sharpens this structure. Yet native digit-token outputs weakly expose it: the unembedding matrix filters the ordinal direction, and the digit-token row space retains below 1.15% across all 16 model-dataset combinations, with a 16 to 77 absolute-point accuracy gap between linear-probe and native outputs. We introduce Ordinal Lens Alignment (OLA), a frozen-backbone inference-time method that trains lightweight W_S-anchored lenses on mid-to-deep decoder layers, fuses them into an ordinal distribution, and corrects only digit-token logits at generation. OLA outperforms the SOTA LoRA-tuned OrderChain baseline in most settings while keeping the MLLM frozen, surpasses discriminative ordinal baselines in most cells, and improves over an offline lens in every setting.

</details>


### [55] [Triangulation-Free Bundle Adjustment with Graduated Non-Convexity for Camera Pose Refinement from Coarse Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21008)
*Nikolaos Kyriazis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种移除三角化步骤的移动AR位姿精化方法，直接对深度沿反投影射线进行优化，避免将先验误差烘焙进结构中。在ScanNet++ iPhone数据集上，该方法在保持先验精度的同时显著提升鲁棒性，远超经典束调整和学习的精化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动AR框架为每张手机拍摄图像附加度量先验位姿，将其转为重建级位姿是视图合成前的关键步骤。发现经典精化器（COLMAP三角化+先验播种的束调整）反而使精确的ARKit先验在所有15个场景中退化（0.55度增至0.74度），原因是先验误差被三角化步骤烘焙进结构。

Method: 核心创新是"无三角化"的求解：每个关键点沿自身反投影射线拥有一个标量深度，每次匹配贡献两个对称的跨投影残差，结构在每个迭代中被重新表达而非预先固定。由于不承诺具体结构，目标函数还支持渐进非凸性（GNC），增强了鲁棒性。

Result: 在ScanNet++上：房间级先验保持在0.57度误差，330次扰动测试零失败；物体级达0.265度/1.80mm（先验0.456度），单CPU中位数10秒/场景，对比学习精化器需2.5 GPU小时。鲁棒性方面，经典方法在1-2度先验误差即崩溃，无方法存活32度；本方法在16度/80mm下425/425全部成功，32度/160mm下85%成功。

Conclusion: 通过消除三角化预提交，方法避免了先验误差的固化，在保持名义精度（与经典束调整相当）的同时大幅提升鲁棒性，并能在没有任何初值假设（无需先验播种）时也能工作。物体尺度精度虽未超越但至少保持在基准噪声底线上。

Abstract: Mobile AR frameworks attach a metric pose prior to every casual phone capture, and turning it into reconstruction-grade poses cheaply on CPU is the step before novel-view synthesis. The least a refiner owes an accurate prior is not to make it worse. The workhorse refiner does. On 15 ScanNet++ iPhone room captures, COLMAP triangulation plus prior-seeded bundle adjustment degrades an accurate ARKit prior in all 15, 0.55 degrees to 0.74 degrees by scene-mean.
  The cause is the seeding. Structure is triangulated from the prior before anything is optimized, so the prior's error is baked into the structure the optimizer trusts. We remove the triangulation. Every keypoint owns a scalar depth along its own back-projected ray and each match contributes two symmetric cross-projection residuals, so structure is re-expressed at every iterate. The same solve holds the room prior at 0.57 degrees and never fails in 330 perturbed room runs, and at object scale reaches 0.265 degrees/1.80 mm from a prior at 0.456 degrees in a median of 10 s per scene on one CPU, against 2.5 GPU-hours for a learned refiner.
  Because no structure is committed, the objective also admits graduated non-convexity, which measures how deep the defect goes. Classical refinement collapses past 1-2 degrees of prior error, barely beyond a real ARKit prior, and no classical refinement arm survives 32 degrees. Ours recovers 425 of 425 runs through 16 degrees/80 mm and 85% at 32 degrees/160 mm, and perturbed rooms through 32 degrees.
  Nominal object-scale accuracy is on par rather than better, on a benchmark at its own noise floor, where classical bundle adjustment is a strong baseline absent from the literature. One scene fails for every solver already at zero perturbation. Re-mapping from position priors matches us in the prior's frame but discards it, so it cannot exploit a prior worth keeping or be warm-started.

</details>


### [56] [Dorsal Hand Images for Immersive (XR) and Privacy-preserving Age Assurance and Child Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21009)
*Riccardo Bovo,George Loukas,Josh P. Davis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用XR头显自带的自我中心摄像头捕获手背图像，作为面部识别的替代方案，用于会话中的连续年龄保证。研究收集了436名跨越未成年-成年边界、按年龄和性别分层且种族多样的参与者的数据集，在18岁法定阈值下评估标准神经网络架构，结果显示系统在挑战-31工作点实现零未成年通过率，且对肤色变化具有稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR环境需要确保年龄适配，但现有年龄保证仅在注册时执行，无法验证会话期间活跃用户的年龄。基于面部的方法在XR中不实用，因为需要摘下头显自拍，既破坏沉浸感又带来面部图像分享的隐私风险，导致XR平台缺乏可行的连续、会话内且隐私保护的年龄保证方案。

Method: 提出以手背作为面部替代的生物特征模态，利用XR头显固有的自我中心摄像头捕获手势交互。收集436名参与者的年龄和性别分层、种族多样数据集，在不受控光照和方向条件下，评估标准神经网络架构在18岁法定阈值下的年龄保证性能。

Result: 在手背图像上，标准神经网络在18岁阈值下表现出稳健的年龄保证能力，性能对肤色变化不敏感。在该数据集上，挑战-31工作点实现零未成年通过率，表明该系统可作为年龄保证的可行一级过滤器。

Conclusion: 手背形态计量学是一种有效且更注重隐私的生物特征模态，适合用于XR环境中的会话内年龄保证，为XR平台提供了一条可行的连续年龄验证路径。

Abstract: Ensuring that Extended Reality (XR) environments are age-appropriate is an important regulatory and safety challenge. However, current age assurance operates only at registration and cannot verify the age of the active user during a session. Face-based approaches, the dominant solution in social media and adult platforms, are impractical in XR, because they require removing the headset and taking a self-captured image, often on a mobile app. This both breaks immersion and introduces the privacy risk of sharing face pictures with third parties, which leaves XR platforms without a viable path to continuous, in-session and privacy-preserving age assurance. We propose the dorsal part of the hand as an alternative to the face, by exploiting the egocentric cameras that XR headsets inherently and naturally use to capture gesture interactions. To evaluate this, we collect an age- and sex-stratified, ethnodiverse dataset of 436 participants spanning the minor--adult boundary, captured under unconstrained lighting and orientation conditions. To characterise what is achievable with off-the-shelf methods at the minor--adult boundary, we evaluate standard neural network architectures for age assurance at the legally critical 18-year threshold. Analysis confirms performance is robust to skin-tone variation. On this dataset, the challenge-31 operating point achieves zero minor admission, making the system a viable first-stage filter for age assurance. These findings position dorsal hand morphometrics as an effective and more privacy-preserving biometric modality for in-session age assurance in XR.

</details>


### [57] [Recognition-Conditioned Reasoning: A Training-Free Multimodal-LLM Pipeline for Fine-Grained Micro-Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21022)
*Fengshun Wang,Jin'ang Han,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个训练免费、仅依赖提示词的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）系统，在MAC 2026微动作挑战赛的细粒度理解赛道（MA-Bench）中获第一名。系统动态将八个子任务路由到经验上最适合的MLLM：判别式MLLM处理封闭式识别任务，生成式MLLM处理开放式描述与推理任务，在开放式任务上平均得分2.68（五分制），远超第二名的1.44。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微动作（micro-actions）是细微、短暂、低幅度的身体动作，能可靠泄露情绪和心理状态。理解它们不仅需要标签，还需要描述哪些身体部位在运动，并推理为什么一个片段值得特定细粒度类别。作者希望在禁止微调和标注监督的条件下构建一个高效系统。

Method: 系统完全基于冻结的MLLM构建，通过动态路由机制将八个子任务分配给经验上最适合的模型——判别式MLLM用于封闭式识别任务，生成式MLLM用于开放式描述和推理任务，全程仅使用提示词，不进行任何训练或微调。

Result: 该系统在MAC 2026微动作挑战赛细粒度理解赛道中获第一名，在开放式任务上平均得分2.68（五分制），显著优于第二名方法的1.44，具有统计学显著性的性能优势。

Conclusion: 任务-模型动态路由策略在无需训练的情况下能够显著提升微动作细粒度理解性能，尤其是开放式描述与推理任务，验证了判别式与生成式MLLM互补分工的有效性。

Abstract: Micro-actions are subtle, short, low-amplitude body movements, such as a fidgeting hand or a slight head tilt, that humans perform with little conscious intent yet that reliably leak emotional and psychological state. Understanding them goes beyond assigning a label: a model must also describe which body parts move and reason, faithfully, about why a clip warrants a particular fine-grained category. We present the training-free, prompt-only system that won first place in the fine-grained understanding track (MA-Bench) of the MAC~2026 Micro-Action Challenge, where both fine-tuning and ground-truth supervision are disallowed. Built entirely upon frozen multimodal large language models (MLLMs), the system dynamically routes each of the eight sub-tasks to the MLLM empirically best suited for that task: a discriminative MLLM for closed-ended recognition tasks and a generative MLLM for open-ended description and reasoning tasks. This architecture achieves a statistically significant performance advantage on open-ended tasks, attaining an average score of 2.68 (on a five-point scale) compared to 1.44 for the second-best approach.

</details>


### [58] [COMET: Contrastive Motion-Enhanced Temporal Reasoning for Video Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21030)
*Chenghua Zhu,Zhaolu Kang,Qifan Shi,Siyan Wu,Kehan Jiang,Lei Wei,Lianyu Hu,Guangyuan Dong,Mingbo Yang,Rui Lu,Guibo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COMET框架，通过显式时间表示、外观-运动融合和方向感知优化来系统性增强视频多模态大语言模型的运动-时间理解能力。实验显示在Qwen3-VL-8B和InternVL2.5-8B上均取得一致的性能提升，且不影响静态感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频多模态大语言模型虽然在整体上取得显著进展，但细粒度的运动-时间理解仍然脆弱。核心瓶颈不仅在于稀疏帧采样，更在于缺乏完整的时间建模流程：缺少显式表示帧间变化、实现外观-运动交互、以及优化时间方向敏感性的能力。

Method: COMET框架包含三个关键组成部分：(1) 基于泰勒帧差分的时序运动分支，显式表示帧间运动证据；(2) 通过时间注意力偏差增强的交叉注意力，将运动信息注入外观流；(3) 结合时间先验蒸馏与前向-反向TC-GRPO阶段，将时序转化为直接学习信号。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上，动作中心任务（STAR、SSv2）平均提升4.9%，时间推理任务（NExT-QA、CLEVRER、LLaVA-178K）较BL-GRPO提升2.1%，而静态感知任务保持不变。相同的增益模式也迁移到InternVL2.5-8B，表明COMET可跨模型家族泛化。

Conclusion: COMET通过完整的时间建模流程（显式时间表示、外观-运动融合、方向感知优化）能够有效增强视频MLLMs的运动-时间理解能力，在动作中心和时间推理任务上带来显著且一致的改进，同时保持静态感知性能不受影响，且在不同模型家族上均具有泛化性。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models have advanced significantly, yet fine-grained motion-temporal understanding remains fragile. The core bottleneck is not only sparse frame sampling, but also the lack of a complete temporal modeling pipeline for explicitly representing frame-to-frame change, enabling appearance-motion interaction, and optimizing temporal direction sensitivity. We propose COMET, a temporally grounded framework that systematically strengthens video MLLMs through explicit temporal representation, appearance-motion fusion, and direction-aware optimization. Architecturally, COMET introduces a temporal motion branch built on Taylor frame differences and injects its motion evidence into the appearance stream via temporal attention bias-enhanced cross-attention. For optimization, COMET combines temporal prior distillation with a forward-reverse TC-GRPO stage that turns temporal order into a direct learning signal and strengthens the model's use of directional motion patterns encoded by the temporal motion branch. The method achieves consistent overall improvements with a pronounced motion-temporal bias: on Qwen3-VL-8B, action-centric tasks (STAR, SSv2) improve by 4.9% on average, temporal reasoning tasks (NExT-QA, CLEVRER, LLaVA-178K) by 2.1% over BL-GRPO, while static perception tasks (PerceptionTest) remain on par. The same gain pattern also transfers to InternVL2.5-8B, indicating that COMET generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [59] [CoST: Semantic-Aware Urban Understanding via Spatial-Temporal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21041)
*Yutian Jiang,Jiabo Liu,Xixuan Hao,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoST，一个基于对比学习的空间-时间框架，通过对齐空间上下文与多时相语义来提取跨区域共享的地理规律，在多个下游任务和未见场景中平均相对提升8.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间表示学习方法在跨区域泛化和语义可解释性方面存在不足，主要原因是依赖区域特定的辅助数据，且忽视了多时相城市影像中的语义对齐问题。

Method: CoST采用对比学习框架，一方面显式建模空间相关性以捕捉可迁移的地理结构，另一方面利用多年城市变化语义将学习到的表示与高层地理语义进行对齐，从而提取跨区域通用的地理规律。

Result: 在多种下游任务和未见场景中，CoST均取得一致的优越性能，在八个城市指标设置下相比最强基线方法平均相对提升8.7%。

Conclusion: CoST通过空间-时间双维度的语义对齐，有效解决了跨区域泛化问题，为大规模城市分析提供了更具可解释性和通用性的地理空间表示学习方法。

Abstract: Geospatial representation learning from satellite imagery is a fundamental problem for large-scale urban analysis and real-world applications. Despite recent advances, current methods struggle with cross-region generalization and semantic interpretability due to their reliance on region-specific auxiliary data and the neglect of semantic alignment within multi-temporal urban imagery. Therefore, we present CoST, a novel \underline{Co}ntrastive-based \underline{S}patial-\underline{T}emporal framework that aligns spatial context with multi-temporal semantics to extract universal geographic regularities shared across regions. Specifically, CoST explicitly models spatial correlations to capture transferable geographic structures and exploits multi-year urban change semantics to align learned representations with high-level geo-semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoST consistently achieves superior performance across various downstream tasks and in unseen scenario, yielding an average relative gain of 8.7\% over the strongest competing methods across eight city-indicator settings. The code is available in \href{https://github.com/Arandinglv/CoST}{this repo}.

</details>


### [60] [CoAnchor: Robust Collaborative Perception under Spatio-Temporal Misalignment via Object-Level Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21055)
*Chi Li,Rui Lin,Aobo Ji,Dongzhu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoAnchor提出了一种以锚点为中心的时空对齐框架，用于异步协同感知。它通过构建稀疏的物体级时空锚点作为共享接口，将空间精修、时间传播和当前时刻验证统一在一个轻量级循环中，联合解决通信延迟和相对位姿噪声带来的问题，在保持干净场景竞争力的同时显著提升延迟和位姿扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知方法通常分别处理通信延迟和相对位姿噪声，缺乏统一且高效的联合解决方案。实际部署中，接收到的协作消息同时受到时延和位姿噪声的影响，导致观测过时、空间错位和特征融合不稳定，需要一种端到端的统一框架同时应对这两个挑战。

Method: CoAnchor构建稀疏的物体级时空锚点作为共享接口，以锚点为中心将空间精修、时间传播和当前时刻验证紧密结合在一个统一循环中，避免直接在稠密的BEV特征上进行推理，从而保持校正过程的轻量高效。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CoAnchor在干净设置下保持竞争力，并在联合延迟和位姿扰动下显著提升鲁棒性，同时实现了良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: CoAnchor验证了以稀疏物体级锚点为中心的时空对齐框架能够统一且高效地解决异步协同感知中的延迟和位姿噪声问题，为实际自动驾驶部署提供了兼顾精度与效率的可行方案。

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends the sensing range of a single vehicle by fusing observations from nearby agents, which improves the robustness of autonomous driving. In realistic deployments, however, the received collaborator messages are often affected by both communication delay and relative-pose noise, which jointly cause stale observations, spatial misalignment, and unstable feature fusion. Existing methods usually address these issues from either the spatial or temporal side, but handling them jointly in a unified and efficient manner remains challenging. In this paper, we propose CoAnchor, an anchor-centric spatio-temporal alignment framework for asynchronous collaborative perception. Instead of directly reasoning on dense BEV features, CoAnchor builds sparse object-level spatio-temporal anchors as a shared interface for pose correction and tightly connects spatial refinement, temporal propagation, and current-time verification within one unified loop, while keeping the overall correction process lightweight. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets illustrate that CoAnchor remains competitive under clean settings and improves the robustness under joint delay and pose perturbations with a favorable practical accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [61] [Robust Validation to Geometric Perturbations for Autonomous Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21066)
*Gregoire Theau,Melanie Ducoffe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉导航与自主降落等安全关键场景，指出了标准梯度攻击方法（如APGD）在姿态估计任务中的失效问题，并提出基于全局Lipschitz优化（GLO）的鲁棒性验证框架，在YOLOv8-Pose+PnP系统上对旋转与对比度扰动进行评估，能够有效定位关键失效模式并缩减超80%的搜索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域中的自主系统需要针对物理上可行的几何扰动（而非抽象的像素噪声）进行鲁棒性保证。现有基于梯度的启发式攻击方法在分类任务中已被证明失效，本文进一步发现这些方法在姿态估计任务中表现甚至不如随机采样基线，因此需要更原则性的鲁棒性验证方法。

Method: 将姿态估计鲁棒性问题重构为全局Lipschitz优化（GLO）框架，利用其理论收敛保证来有效定位全局最优解，实现对鲁棒性的严格验证。在YOLOv8-Pose关键点检测器与PnP求解器上，对旋转和对比度扰动进行评估。

Result: GLO成功识别出位置偏差超过安全运行限制的关键失效模式，同时将搜索空间快速剪枝超过80%。这是首个将几何鲁棒性验证扩展到连续关键点回归和深度目标检测任务的研究。

Conclusion: GLO为重梯度的姿态估计鲁棒性验证提供了有原则且高效的替代方案，是迈向可认证的鲁棒自主感知系统的实际一步。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous systems in safety-critical domains demands guaranteed robustness against physically plausible geometric perturbations rather than abstract pixel-wise noise. In vision-based navigation and autonomous landing, machine learning components require rigorous validation under dynamic operational conditions such as camera rotations and lighting shifts. Extending findings on the failure of first-order spatial attacks in classification, we show that standard gradient-based heuristics (e.g. APGD) similarly fail on for pose estimation, often performing worse than a simple random sampling baseline.
  To overcome these optimization bottlenecks, we reformulate pose estimation robustness within the framework of Global Lipschitzian Optimization (GLO). We argue that GLO offers a principled approach to robust validation, effectively localizing global optima with strong theoretical convergence guarantees. We evaluate this framework on a YOLOv8-Pose keypoint detector with a Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solver against rotation and contrast. In our evaluations, GLO successfully isolates critical failure modes where position deviations exceed safe operational limits, while rapidly pruning the search space by over 80%. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to extend geometric robustness validation to continuous keypoint regression and deep object detection, establishing a practical step toward certifying robust autonomous perception.

</details>


### [62] [AT-ViT: Area-Targeted Multi-View Vision Transformer with Cross-Attention and Multi-Scale Patching for Plant Trait Recognition in Herbarium Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21067)
*Amani Sedrat,Takieddine Chehhat,Youcef Sklab,Hanane Ariouat,Abderrazak Sebaa,Eric Chenin,Jean-Daniel Zucker,Edi Profiti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AT-ViT，一种双分支视觉Transformer模型，通过联合编码原始标本扫描图像与分割掩码衍生图像，并引入掩码引导的补丁加权机制，有效缓解背景元素导致的捷径学习问题，在植物性状识别任务上显著提升精度、注意力定位能力和背景扰动鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物标本图像中的背景元素（如文字标签、装订痕迹、色卡）会导致模型依赖非植物线索进行判别，产生捷径学习，降低泛化能力和可解释性，需要一种能引导模型聚焦植物形态本身的方法。

Method: AT-ViT采用双分支视觉Transformer架构，同时编码原始标本扫描图和分割衍生图；通过多尺度多视角交叉注意力融合方案整合两分支信息；并引入掩码引导的补丁加权机制，放大植物相关区域特征、衰减背景特征，使模型学习植物中心化的表征。

Result: 在多个性状分类任务（如叶基部形状、刺）上，AT-ViT持续提升准确率；空间注意力对齐度大幅提升（Avg IoU_p提高15.66-18.03个百分点，Avg IoU_b降低27.92-31.02个百分点）；在背景扰动下，比ResNet101高多达32.32个准确率点，比CrossViT高多达5.07个点。

Conclusion: 掩码引导的双分支Transformer架构能有效抑制背景捷径学习，使模型更关注植物器官本身，在精度、可解释性和鲁棒性三方面均优于基线方法，为植物性状自动化识别提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Automated plant traits recognition from herbarium images is essential for plant sciences, yet remains challenging because background elements (e.g., textual labels, mounting artifacts, and color charts) can introduce shortcut learning, leading models to rely on spurious non-plant cues rather than plant morphology. This bias degrades both generalization and interpretability. In this paper, we introduce AT-ViT, a dual-branch Vision Transformer that jointly encodes raw herbarium scans and their segmented-derived counterparts via a multi-scale, multi-view cross-attention fusion scheme. AT-ViT further incorporates a mask-guided patch weighting mechanism that amplifies plant-relevant regions and attenuates background-driven features. By learning from the original scans while being guided by segmentation masks through the mask-guided patch reweighting mechanism, the model is encouraged to focus on plant organs and learn plant-centric representations more effectively. Across multiple trait classification tasks (e.g., leaf base shape, thorns), AT-ViT delivers consistent accuracy gains, improves attention localization on plant regions, and exhibits increased robustness under synthetic background perturbations. Specifically, AT-ViT substantially improves spatial attention grounding, boosting plant-region alignment (Avg IoU_p: +15.66 to +18.03 pp) while reducing background overlap (Avg IoU_b: -27.92 to -31.02 pp) relative to CrossViT, and remains markedly more robust to background perturbations, outperforming ResNet101 by up to +32.32 accuracy points and CrossViT by up to +5.07 points under background-noise conditions.

</details>


### [63] [Gaussian-Mixture Latent Flow for Stochastic 3D Human Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21093)
*Yue Ma,Frederick W. B. Li,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在流模型的人体运动预测方法，采用数据驱动的混合高斯先验来更好地解耦多样化的人类行为，并通过模型的可逆性实现自然的不确定性量化，在Human3.6M和AMASS数据集上达到准确性和合理性的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机人体运动预测方法虽在准确性和多样性上表现良好，但忽视了物理合理性（预测结果可能不真实）和不确定性量化，这两者对于实际应用和下游任务至关重要。

Method: 提出一种潜在流模型，配备数据驱动的混合高斯先验，通过从训练数据中提取模式而非依赖额外标注来更有效地解耦多样化的人类行为；利用模型完全可逆的特性，通过易于处理的似然计算实现自然的不确定性量化。

Result: 在Human3.6M和AMASS数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确性和合理性两方面均达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法同时解决了现有随机人体运动预测方法在合理性和不确定性量化方面的不足，证明了数据驱动的混合高斯先验与潜在流模型结合的有效性。

Abstract: Stochastic human motion prediction aims to forecast future motion distributions. Although recent studies have achieved strong performance in terms of accuracy and diversity, they often overlook plausibility (e.g., resulting in physically unrealistic predictions) and uncertainty quantification, both of which are essential for real-world applications and downstream tasks. To address these issues, we propose a latent flow-based model equipped with a data-driven Gaussian mixture prior that more effectively disentangles diverse human behaviors than conventional single-modal priors. This prior is derived from patterns in the training data without requiring additional annotations. Furthermore, the fully invertible nature of our model enables natural uncertainty quantification through tractable likelihood computation. Experiments on the Human3.6M and AMASS datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in both accuracy and plausibility.

</details>


### [64] [When does fusing hand-crafted knowledge with learned representations pay? A cost-normalized benchmark of stacking, substitution, and interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21098)
*Ahmad AlMughrabi,Albert Clop,Benjamin Busam,Ricardo Marques,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了将手工先验知识（固定的Gabor滤波器组）与数据驱动学习融合的收益、冗余和风险。在统一实验协议下，跨越13个数据集、9个骨干网络和多种数据驱动替代方案（SimCLR、SimSiam、DINO、ImageNet迁移、增强、学习型教师），发现三种反复出现的结论：不同"货币"来源可叠加（先验与DeiT增强在注意力骨干上组合可提升+26点）；相同"货币"来源互相替代（与有效自监督预训练组合从不优于单一最优源）；过强融合会干扰已有初始化（ImageNet迁移损失-15到-17点）。冻结特征诊断虽不能前瞻预测结果，但能事后分离这些结论，且能在实际标签预算下以0.17点精度预测端到端增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺场景中，将先验知识与数据驱动学习融合具有吸引力，但缺乏受控实验来明确说明何时有帮助、何时冗余或有害。现有研究缺乏统一的基准来系统回答这一问题。

Method: 采用统一的冻结实验配方，固定一个手工先验知识源（仅在训练时注入约2%开销的Gabor滤波器银行），对比数据驱动替代方案（SimCLR、SimSiam、DINO、ImageNet迁移、增强、学习型教师）。覆盖13个数据集、9种骨干网络、150到128万张图像、32-224像素、250万-8600万参数，包含分类、分割和检测任务。

Result: 三种结果反复出现：1）不同货币来源可叠加：先验与DeiT增强在注意力骨干上组合，ViT-B/16在224像素下提升+26点，双倍预算下+6.7点；2）相同货币来源相互替代：与有效自监督预训练组合从不优于单一最优源；3）全强度融合已信息化的初始化会造成干扰：ImageNet迁移损失-15至-17点，弱化辅助权重可消除。冻结特征诊断可事后分离结果但不能前瞻预测（9个未见对中仅猜对1个）；但可预测端到端增益至0.17点精度（30个单元、7个数据集），分解Δ=G+readout(base)在符号上成立。

Conclusion: 先验知识与数据驱动学习的融合效果取决于"货币"类型：不同来源的信息可互补叠加，相同来源的信息则互相替代，过强融合会伤害已有初始化。虽然冻结特征诊断无法前瞻预测三类结果，但可以在实践者自身的标签预算下可靠预测端到端增益，为在数据稀缺场景中决策是否及如何融合先验知识提供实用指导。

Abstract: Fusing prior knowledge with data-driven learning is attractive where data is scarce, yet no controlled account says when it helps, is redundant, or harms. We benchmark one fixed hand-crafted knowledge source, a pinned bank of Gabor targets injected only during training at $\sim$2\% overhead, against data-driven alternatives (SimCLR, SimSiam, DINO, ImageNet transfer, augmentation, learned teachers) under one frozen recipe with fixed subsets: 13 datasets, 9 backbones, 150 to 1.28M images, 32--224\,px, 2.5M--86M parameters ($\computeCells$ classification configurations over $\computeRuns$ runs, plus segmentation and detection transplants). Across the training-time combinations we measure, three outcomes recur (decision-level fusion differs). Different-\emph{currency} sources can stack: the prior composes with DeiT augmentation on attention backbones and is worth $+26$ points to ViT-B/16 at $224$\,px, $+6.7$ at twice that budget. Same-currency sources substitute: against effective self-supervised pretraining, the combination never usefully exceeds the better single source. Fusing at full strength into an already-informed initialization interferes in proportion to what it carries: ImageNet transfer, $-15$ to $-17$ points, removed by a weaker auxiliary weight. Frozen-feature diagnostics measured on each source alone separate these outcomes retrospectively but do not predict them: a rule built on them calls one of nine unseen pairs. At a practitioner's own label budget, the frozen-feature gain predicts the end-to-end gain to within $0.17$ points across 30 cells and seven datasets; the underlying decomposition, $Δ= G + \readout(\mathrm{base})$, holds in sign on $\auditRate\%$ of testable cells and is called an unseen backbone family's feature gain in advance. The project page is https://amughrabi.github.io/MomentAux.

</details>


### [65] [A2DINOv3: Rethinking Multi-Modal Object Detection via Socialized Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21099)
*Jiekang Feng,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Xinjie Yao,Yueying Zhang,Yike Gao,Ranxin Li,Guanzuo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出A2DINOv3，一种基于社交化协作协议的多专家协作框架，通过选择性、受限的跨模态交互和零初始化策略，将DINOv3适配到多模态目标检测任务，在四个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集跨模态融合策略迫使异构模态无差别交互，会引入冗余信息并破坏预训练表征。如何在不损害DINOv3等视觉基础模型预训练先验的前提下实现有效的多模态融合是核心挑战。

Method: 提出多专家协作框架A2DINOv3，包含社交化协作协议（SCP），将RGB和红外分支建模为异构专家，各自保留专业知识的同时通过选择性、受限交互交换互补信息；并引入零初始化策略，使跨模态协作从模态特定学习平滑过渡到协作表征学习。

Result: 在四个多模态基准（GAIIC航拍、FLIR自动驾驶、LLVIP低光监控、M3FD多样真实场景）上均取得最先进的检测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性约束的跨模态交互能有效缓解有害干扰并保护预训练先验，为多模态目标检测提供了一种更优的适配策略。

Abstract: Multi-modal object detection is essential for robust scene understanding in challenging conditions, including low-light and adverse environments. Recent vision foundation models (e.g., DINOv3) have exhibited strong representation capabilities, yet adapting them to multi-modal scenarios remains challenging. Existing dense cross-modal fusion strategies often force heterogeneous modalities to interact indiscriminately, which may introduce redundant information and disrupt the valuable pre-trained representations.
